Hi, doctor. It's really nice to meet you too. Thank you so much for fitting me in. 

Um, well, I hope I'm explaining this right, but I guess I'm here because I've been having a little bit of trouble at work lately. It's probably nothing major, but... I just can't seem to keep my mind on one thing anymore. Well, um... I guess maybe a little bit? I mean, warehouses are just noisy places anyway, right? There's always a beeper going off, or a forklift alarm, or someone dropping a pallet. 

I wouldn't want you to think I'm bad at my job, because I really try to be so careful, but... lately, those little sounds just kind of pull my attention away. That's actually kind of why I thought I should come in. I just can't seem to keep my mind on one thing like I usually do. I hope I'm not making it sound worse than it is... I'm sure it's just an adjustment period and I'm probably making a bigger deal out of it than I should be. Does that sound like a normal reaction to a change in routine like that? I just want to make sure I'm giving you the right kind of information.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel this started because of your husband stopping work and the change in your routine?\n\nPatient: Well, um... I really don't know exactly why it started. I'm sorry, I hope it's okay that I don't have a better answer for you.
